# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
Gramian angular field mosaic, 
minute level correlates -> every minute for 24 hours ^NUrctc0k

Start with raw (but calibrated) triaxial data 80Hz ^IZH9rJ28

There will be >6 of these per participant ^PysTMMoU

BiLSTM Encoder Layers ^8FLc2jnR

Lowest Latent Representation ^x022AGCo

AE tries to recreate the original image ^P7jdknE2

3 Channel vectors for each subject for total n subjects ^0v7ETvnA

iterate and run PCA to find optimal # of vectors for each axis for each individual ^K39mx74S

Cache hard compute and iterate to find optimal # of clusters ^P3DGYmss

Run OLS, does the data itself explain characteristics? ^0Tmli29L

Great stop here & report ^YqxZOv28

- If doesn't improve, report that cutpoints are necessary. ^poYGrqXM
